Company Overview\n\n**Company Name:** Nexus Clinical LLC  \n**Founded:** 2009  \n**Headquarters:** 7495 W Atlantic Ave, Unit 281, Delray Beach, FL 33446 (previously listed as Naples, FL and Weston, FL at various points -- the company may have relocated or has multiple addresses)  \n**Phone (Sales):** (239) 204-4643  \n**Phone (Support):** (239) 204-4642  \n**Email:** sales@nexusclinical.com  \n**Website:** https://www.nexusclinical.com/  \n**Company Size:** Small -- LinkedIn reports 2-10 employees; a third-party source estimated approximately 19 employees across 2 continents (Asia and North America) as of 2021  \n**Revenue:** Not publicly disclosed  \n\n### Leadership Team\n- **Pooja Tripathi** -- Chief Executive Officer (25+ years experience; with company since inception, CEO since 2018)\n- **Kiran Agate** -- Vice President, Information Technology (20+ years in software design/development; led EHR design and development; listed as CHPL contact: kiran.agate@nexusclinical.com)\n- **Jennifer Somers** -- Billing Consultant, Healthcare Subject Matter Expert (20+ years in healthcare; dual AAPC certifications: CPC and CPPM)\n- **Sunil Gupta** -- Director, Customer Acquisition & Services (20+ years in business development; 15+ in Healthcare IT)\n\nCombined leadership experience: 125+ years. The company positions itself as delivering \"the lowest possible prices, with the best accessible software solution.\"\n\n### Mission\nDevelop and deliver Healthcare IT solutions to ambulatory medical practices. The company evolved from a focused EHR product into a complete medical platform encompassing EHR, Practice Management, Medical Billing, Patient Portal, Telehealth, eFax, and more.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Product Overview: Nexus EHR\n\n**Product Name:** Nexus EHR  \n**Current Certified Version:** 7.3  \n**Deployment:** Cloud-based (SaaS), accessible via any web browser or tablet  \n**Target Market:** Ambulatory practices -- solo practitioners through organizations of 50+ physicians  \n**Intended Users:** Doctors, nurses, lab managers, pharmacists, clinical staff, billing staff, front desk  \n\nNexus EHR is described as \"an intuitive, cloud-based EHR that works the way you practice.\" It is a unified platform combining clinical documentation, practice management, billing, patient engagement, and telehealth into a single system.\n\n---\n\n## 3. ONC Certification Details\n\n**CHPL Product Number:** 15.04.04.2989.Nexu.07.03.1.221227  \n**CHPL ID:** 11130  \n**Certification Date:** December 27, 2022  \n**Certification Status:** Active  \n**Certifying Body:** Drummond Group (ONC-ACB)  \n**Edition:** 2015 Cures Update  \n**Intended User Description:** Ambulatory  \n\n### Certified Criteria (34 total)\nThe product is certified against the following 170.315 criteria:\n\n**Clinical (a) criteria:**\n- (a)(1) -- Computerized Provider Order Entry (CPOE) -- Medications\n- (a)(2) -- CPOE -- Laboratory\n- (a)(3) -- CPOE -- Diagnostic Imaging\n- (a)(4) -- Drug-Drug, Drug-Allergy Interaction Checks\n- (a)(5) -- Demographics\n- (a)(12) -- Family Health History\n- (a)(14) -- Implantable Device List\n\n**Care Coordination (b) criteria:**\n- (b)(1) -- Transitions of Care\n- (b)(2) -- Clinical Information Reconciliation and Incorporation\n- (b)(3) -- Electronic Prescribing\n- (b)(10) -- Electronic Health Information Export\n- (b)(11) -- (not listed in standard descriptions -- likely a newer criterion)\n\n**Clinical Quality Measures (c) criteria:**\n- (c)(1) -- Clinical Quality Measures -- Record and Export\n- (c)(2) -- Clinical Quality Measures -- Import and Calculate\n- (c)(3) -- Clinical Quality Measures -- Report\n\n**Privacy and Security (d) criteria:**\n- (d)(1) -- Authentication, Access Control, Authorization\n- (d)(2) -- Auditable Events and Tamper-Resistance\n- (d)(3) -- Audit Report(s)\n- (d)(4) -- Amendments\n- (d)(5) -- Automatic Access Time-out\n- (d)(6) -- Emergency Access\n- (d)(7) -- End-User Device Encryption\n- (d)(8) -- Integrity\n- (d)(9) -- Trusted Connection\n- (d)(12) -- Encrypt Authentication Credentials\n- (d)(13) -- Multi-Factor Authentication\n\n**Patient Engagement (e) criteria:**\n- (e)(1) -- View, Download, and Transmit to 3rd Party\n- (e)(3) -- Patient Health Information Capture\n\n**Public Health (f) criteria:**\n- (f)(1) -- Transmission to Immunization Registries\n- (f)(2) -- Transmission to Public Health Agencies -- Syndromic Surveillance\n- (f)(5) -- Transmission to Public Health Agencies -- Electronic Case Reporting\n\n**Standardized API (g) criteria:**\n- (g)(2) -- Automated Numerator Recording\n- (g)(3) -- Safety-Enhanced Design\n- (g)(4) -- Quality Management System\n- (g)(5) -- Accessibility-Centered Design\n- (g)(6) -- Consolidated CDA Creation Performance\n- (g)(7) -- Application Access -- Patient Selection\n- (g)(9) -- Application Access -- All Data Request\n- (g)(10) -- Standardized API for Patient and Population Services (FHIR)\n\n### CQMs Supported\n20 clinical quality measures (CMS22v5 through CMS167v5)\n\n### Included Components\n- MedlinePlus Connect (patient education)\n- NewCropRx (pharmacy integration, e-prescribing)\n\n### Available Add-ons\n- EMR Direct (interoperability engine)\n- DynaMed Plus by EBSCO Host (clinical decision support)\n\n### MFA\n- Optional for standard login (available via access codes upon request)\n- Mandatory for EPCS (controlled substance prescribing) via mobile push tokens or hardware tokens\n\n---\n\n## 4. Modules and Features\n\n### 4.1 Electronic Health Records (Core EHR)\n\n**Clinical Documentation:**\n- Single-screen visit documentation (no window-switching required)\n- Multi-modal input: preconfigured templates, free text, voice-to-text (built-in), Dragon compatibility, transcription services\n- Support for up to 10 simultaneous patient records open without losing unsaved work\n- Rapid charting with conventional formats (superbill, medical history, review of systems)\n- Customizable workflows per visit type\n- Real-time alerts for pending quality measures before chart sign-off (MIPS/MACRA)\n- Contextual screens for charting, lab results, reporting, and orders\n\n**Clinical Integration:**\n- Integrated DICOM/PACS system (medical imaging)\n- eLab and diagnostics center interfaces\n- Plug-and-play camera device compatibility\n- Direct messaging capabilities\n- eRx, EPCS, PDMP, and SureScripts drug history integration (via NewCrop)\n\n**Document Management:**\n- Paperless conversion of administrative and clinical forms\n- Scan, upload, and store documents on Nexus Cloud\n- eFax module for sending/receiving faxes within the platform\n- Electronic signature pad functionality\n- Online patient intake and consent form submission\n- Custom forms and letter templates with auto-fill\n\n### 4.2 Practice Management\n\n- Intuitive scheduling system with configurable appointment types\n- Automated appointment reminders (email/text) to reduce no-shows\n- Patient recalls and follow-up scheduling\n- Task management\n- Real-time eligibility checks and payment collection at check-in\n- Digital patient intake (contact info, medical history, allergies, visit reasons, consent)\n- Document scanning, importing, and organizing with custom classification\n- Inventory management (track in-house medications and supplies; prescribe and bill directly)\n- Integrated eFax for sending and importing documents\n- Dashboard for operations oversight\n\n### 4.3 Medical Billing\n\n- Seamless integration with EHR clinical documentation\n- Configurable superbill, fee schedules, and claim workflows\n- Real-time eligibility verification\n- Claims submission and management\n- Revenue cycle management\n- Error minimization in billing processes\n- Timely filing adherence to prevent claim denials\n- Coordinated workflow between front desk, clinicians, and billing staff\n\n### 4.4 Patient Engagement / Patient Portal\n\n- 24/7 access to health information via portal\n- Review visit summaries and medical documentation\n- View and download medical records\n- View account statements\n- Update demographics, medical history, and allergies\n- Complete intake forms and custom health questionnaires\n- Request appointments online\n- Join telemedicine/eVisit sessions\n- Pay balances through secure online payments\n- Receive automated appointment reminders\n- Complete post-visit experience surveys\n- Secure messaging with providers\n- Online patient registration for new patients\n- Practice-controlled screening of patient-submitted data before acceptance\n\n### 4.5 Telehealth\n\n- Powered by Google Meet\n- Cross-platform: iOS, Android, desktop\n- Patients start eVisit from link in text or email\n- Providers can chart simultaneously during video visit\n- Integrated with scheduling workflow (no duplicate check-in processes)\n- Automated email/text reminders before visits\n- HIPAA-compliant secure infrastructure\n- Eligible for reimbursement when properly documented\n\n### 4.6 E-Prescribing\n\n- Electronic prescribing via NewCrop integration\n- EPCS (Electronic Prescribing for Controlled Substances)\n- PDMP (Prescription Drug Monitoring Program) integration\n- SureScripts drug history integration\n- Formulary checking\n- Drug-drug and drug-allergy interaction checks (ONC certified)\n\n### 4.7 Secure Cloud Infrastructure\n\n- Cloud-based architecture with 24/7 access\n- HIPAA-aligned security\n- Encryption in transit and at rest\n- Audit logs and role-based access control\n- Automated backup systems\n\n---\n\n## 5. Interoperability and Data Exchange\n\n### Standards Supported\n- **FHIR** -- Standardized API for Patient and Population Services (certified under g(10))\n- **HL7** -- For data exchange with practice management systems and other clinical systems\n- **C-CDA** (Consolidated Clinical Document Architecture) -- For transitions of care and document exchange\n- **Custom APIs** -- Nexus offers custom APIs and works with vendor APIs for real-time data exchange\n\n### Interoperability Capabilities\n- Compatible with third-party practice management systems via HL7 or C-CDA file exchange\n- Integration with external hospitals, laboratories, and diagnostic centers\n- Direct messaging for secure clinical communications\n- Public health reporting (immunization registries, syndromic surveillance, electronic case reporting)\n- Electronic Health Information Export (certified under b(10))\n- Patient data view, download, transmit (certified under e(1))\n- Payment processor integration (Stripe, Clover)\n\n### Real World Testing\nNexus maintains RWT documentation for CY2022 through CY2025 (most recent report updated February 2026), demonstrating ongoing compliance with ONC interoperability requirements.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Supported Specialties\n\nNexus EHR offers customizable templates for 25+ medical specialties. Explicitly named specialties include:\n\n1. Cardiology\n2. Family Medicine\n3. General Surgery\n4. Neurology\n5. Neurosurgery\n6. Orthopedics\n7. Podiatry\n8. Psychiatry\n9. Pain Management\n10. Behavioral Health\n11. Dermatology\n12. Pediatrics\n13. Urgent Care\n14. Pulmonary\n15. Oncology\n16. Primary Care\n17. Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery (per testimonial)\n18. Sleep Medicine (per testimonial -- Costrini Sleep Services)\n\nEach specialty gets out-of-the-box templates that can be further customized. The vendor claims to be \"still adding more\" specialties.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Data Stored in the System\n\nBased on the certified criteria, module descriptions, and feature documentation, Nexus EHR stores the following categories of patient and clinical data:\n\n**Patient Demographics:** Name, date of birth, sex, race, ethnicity, preferred language, address, phone, email, insurance information\n\n**Clinical Records:**\n- Chief complaint and history of present illness\n- Medical history (past medical, surgical, family, social)\n- Review of systems\n- Physical examination findings\n- Assessment and plan\n- Problem lists\n- Medication lists (active and historical)\n- Allergy lists\n- Immunization records\n- Vital signs\n- Lab results (via eLab interfaces)\n- Diagnostic imaging (DICOM/PACS integrated)\n- Implantable device list (certified under a(14))\n- Family health history (certified under a(12))\n\n**Orders:**\n- Medication orders (CPOE)\n- Laboratory orders (CPOE)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders (CPOE)\n- Prescriptions (eRx/EPCS)\n\n**Documents:**\n- Clinical notes and visit summaries\n- Scanned/uploaded documents\n- Faxes (sent and received)\n- Consent forms\n- Patient intake forms\n- Letters and custom forms\n- Education documents\n\n**Financial/Administrative:**\n- Superbill/encounter charges\n- Claims data\n- Fee schedules\n- Eligibility verification records\n- Payment records\n- Account statements\n- Appointment schedules and history\n\n**Quality/Compliance:**\n- Clinical quality measure data (20 CQMs)\n- MIPS/MACRA quality reporting data\n- Audit logs\n- Public health reporting data (immunizations, syndromic surveillance, case reporting)\n\n**Patient-Generated:**\n- Portal-submitted demographics updates\n- Health questionnaire responses\n- Appointment requests\n- Secure messages\n- Post-visit surveys\n- Online payments\n\n---\n\n## 8. Pricing\n\n- **Starting Price:** $275/month per NPI (National Provider Identifier), with some sources listing $299/month\n- **Implementation Fee:** Approximately $2,500 one-time for small practices (1-5 doctors)\n- **Contract:** No lock-in period; month-to-month invoicing available\n- **Pricing Model:** Subscription-based; varies by organization size, features needed, and customization level\n- **Positioning:** Marketed as an affordable option compared to competitors\n\n---\n\n## 9. Customer Feedback and Reviews\n\n### Third-Party Rating\n- **Software Finder:** 4.6/5 stars (19 reviews, 95% positive)\n- **Capterra/GetApp:** Generally positive (exact star rating could not be extracted due to access restrictions, but search snippets indicate positive reception)\n\n### Strengths (Consistently Cited)\n1. **Customer Support** -- This is the most universally praised aspect. Users describe response times of ~2 hours, ability to reach someone by phone immediately, professional and courteous staff\n2. **Ease of Use** -- User-friendly, intuitive interface; tabs easy to find and navigate\n3. **Customization** -- Highly customizable templates for specific practice and specialty needs\n4. **Implementation** -- Quick setup (faster than industry standard of ~3 months); thorough pre-transition analysis; excellent on-site go-live support\n5. **No Contract** -- Month-to-month invoicing appreciated\n6. **Built-in PACS** -- Valued by practices needing imaging\n7. **Cloud Accessibility** -- Access from anywhere on any device\n\n### Weaknesses (Cited in Reviews)\n1. **Patient Portal** -- Described as difficult for patients to understand/use\n2. **Lab Ordering** -- Reported as too complicated\n3. **Calendar Sync** -- Users report inability to sync calendar\n4. **UI Quirk** -- Hovering near side tabs accidentally opens them\n5. **Lag** -- Occasional lag between Nexus and billing/scheduling; occasional lag with e-prescribing\n6. **Comparison** -- Some users note it does not match up to larger competitors like Athena Health or Simple Practice\n7. **Learning Curve** -- Some reports of a steep learning curve for new users\n\n### Notable Testimonials from Vendor Website\n- **Dr. Mathew Sipple** (Psychiatry, Bay City Psychiatry): \"Extremely satisfied with the work of Nexus Support\"\n- **Don Causey** (VP/COO, Costrini Sleep Services, Savannah, GA): \"Initial integration between these two systems was almost flawless\"\n- **Dr. Edward Rhomberg** (Orthopedics, Western Arkansas): \"Customer support by the Nexus team is second to none\"\n- **Dr. Brian K. Nadolne** (FAAFP): Ideal for small practices (1-3 physicians); easy meaningful use attestation\n- **Joan Troy** (Practice Administrator, North Atlanta Surgical Associates): Excellent on-site go-live support\n- **George McNeill** (IT Manager, Fortanasce & Associates Neurology, Arcadia, CA): Built-in PACS, data migration assistance, continuous improvement based on suggestions\n- **Dr. Georgiy Brusovanik** (Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery): \"Nexus Clinical has tied it all together for my practice\"\n\n---\n\n## 10. Competitive Positioning\n\nNexus EHR is a small-company product targeting small to midsize ambulatory practices. It competes against much larger players (Athena Health, eClinicalWorks, NextGen, etc.) primarily on:\n- **Price** (lower cost than many competitors)\n- **Customer service** (high-touch, responsive support that large vendors often lack)\n- **Customization** (willingness to tailor the system to individual practice needs)\n- **No long-term contracts**\n\nThe company is small (~10-19 employees) which limits its market reach but enables the personalized service that users repeatedly praise.\n\n---\n\n## 11. criteria:**\n- (g)(2) -- Automated Numerator Recording\n- (g)(3) -- Safety-Enhanced Design\n- (g)(4) -- Quality Management System\n- (g)(5) -- Accessibility-Centered Design\n- (g)(6) -- Consolidated CDA Creation Performance\n- (g)(7) -- Application Access -- Patient Selection\n- (g)(9) -- Application Access -- All Data Request\n- (g)(10) -- Standardized API for Patient and Population Services (FHIR)\n\n### CQMs Supported\n20 clinical quality measures (CMS22v5 through CMS167v5)\n\n### Included Components\n- MedlinePlus Connect (patient education)\n- NewCropRx (pharmacy integration, e-prescribing)\n\n### Available Add-ons\n- EMR Direct (interoperability engine)\n- DynaMed Plus by EBSCO Host (clinical decision support)\n\n### MFA\n- Optional for standard login (available via access codes upon request)\n- Mandatory for EPCS (controlled substance prescribing) via mobile push tokens or hardware tokens\n\n---\n\n## 4. Modules and Features\n\n### 4.1 Electronic Health Records (Core EHR)\n\n**Clinical Documentation:**\n- Single-screen visit documentation (no window-switching required)\n- Multi-modal input: preconfigured templates, free text, voice-to-text (built-in), Dragon compatibility, transcription services\n- Support for up to 10 simultaneous patient records open without losing unsaved work\n- Rapid charting with conventional formats (superbill, medical history, review of systems)\n- Customizable workflows per visit type\n- Real-time alerts for pending quality measures before chart sign-off (MIPS/MACRA)\n- Contextual screens for charting, lab results, reporting, and orders\n\n**Clinical Integration:**\n- Integrated DICOM/PACS system (medical imaging)\n- eLab and diagnostics center interfaces\n- Plug-and-play camera device compatibility\n- Direct messaging capabilities\n- eRx, EPCS, PDMP, and SureScripts drug history integration (via NewCrop)\n\n**Document Management:**\n- Paperless conversion of administrative and clinical forms\n- Scan, upload, and store documents on Nexus Cloud\n- eFax module for sending/receiving faxes within the platform\n- Electronic signature pad functionality\n- Online patient intake and consent form submission\n- Custom forms and letter templates with auto-fill\n\n### 4.2 Practice Management\n\n- Intuitive scheduling system with configurable appointment types\n- Automated appointment reminders (email/text) to reduce no-shows\n- Patient recalls and follow-up scheduling\n- Task management\n- Real-time eligibility checks and payment collection at check-in\n- Digital patient intake (contact info, medical history, allergies, visit reasons, consent)\n- Document scanning, importing, and organizing with custom classification\n- Inventory management (track in-house medications and supplies; prescribe and bill directly)\n- Integrated eFax for sending and importing documents\n- Dashboard for operations oversight\n\n### 4.3 Medical Billing\n\n- Seamless integration with EHR clinical documentation\n- Configurable superbill, fee schedules, and claim workflows\n- Real-time eligibility verification\n- Claims submission and management\n- Revenue cycle management\n- Error minimization in billing processes\n- Timely filing adherence to prevent claim denials\n- Coordinated workflow between front desk, clinicians, and billing staff\n\n### 4.4 Patient Engagement / Patient Portal\n\n- 24/7 access to health information via portal\n- Review visit summaries and medical documentation\n- View and download medical records\n- View account statements\n- Update demographics, medical history, and allergies\n- Complete intake forms and custom health questionnaires\n- Request appointments online\n- Join telemedicine/eVisit sessions\n- Pay balances through secure online payments\n- Receive automated appointment reminders\n- Complete post-visit experience surveys\n- Secure messaging with providers\n- Online patient registration for new patients\n- Practice-controlled screening of patient-submitted data before acceptance\n\n### 4.5 Telehealth\n\n- Powered by Google Meet\n- Cross-platform: iOS, Android, desktop\n- Patients start eVisit from link in text or email\n- Providers can chart simultaneously during video visit\n- Integrated with scheduling workflow (no duplicate check-in processes)\n- Automated email/text reminders before visits\n- HIPAA-compliant secure infrastructure\n- Eligible for reimbursement when properly documented\n\n### 4.6 E-Prescribing\n\n- Electronic prescribing via NewCrop integration\n- EPCS (Electronic Prescribing for Controlled Substances)\n- PDMP (Prescription Drug Monitoring Program) integration\n- SureScripts drug history integration\n- Formulary checking\n- Drug-drug and drug-allergy interaction checks (ONC certified)\n\n### 4.7 Secure Cloud Infrastructure\n\n- Cloud-based architecture with 24/7 access\n- HIPAA-aligned security\n- Encryption in transit and at rest\n- Audit logs and role-based access control\n- Automated backup systems\n\n---\n\n## 5. Interoperability and Data Exchange\n\n### Standards Supported\n- **FHIR** -- Standardized API for Patient and Population Services (certified under g(10))\n- **HL7** -- For data exchange with practice management systems and other clinical systems\n- **C-CDA** (Consolidated Clinical Document Architecture) -- For transitions of care and document exchange\n- **Custom APIs** -- Nexus offers custom APIs and works with vendor APIs for real-time data exchange\n\n### Interoperability Capabilities\n- Compatible with third-party practice management systems via HL7 or C-CDA file exchange\n- Integration with external hospitals, laboratories, and diagnostic centers\n- Direct messaging for secure clinical communications\n- Public health reporting (immunization registries, syndromic surveillance, electronic case reporting)\n- Electronic Health Information Export (certified under b(10))\n- Patient data view, download, transmit (certified under e(1))\n- Payment processor integration (Stripe, Clover)\n\n### Real World Testing\nNexus maintains RWT documentation for CY2022 through CY2025 (most recent report updated February 2026), demonstrating ongoing compliance with ONC interoperability requirements.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Stored in the System\n\nBased on the certified criteria, module descriptions, and feature documentation, Nexus EHR stores the following categories of patient and clinical data:\n\n**Patient Demographics:** Name, date of birth, sex, race, ethnicity, preferred language, address, phone, email, insurance information\n\n**Clinical Records:**\n- Chief complaint and history of present illness\n- Medical history (past medical, surgical, family, social)\n- Review of systems\n- Physical examination findings\n- Assessment and plan\n- Problem lists\n- Medication lists (active and historical)\n- Allergy lists\n- Immunization records\n- Vital signs\n- Lab results (via eLab interfaces)\n- Diagnostic imaging (DICOM/PACS integrated)\n- Implantable device list (certified under a(14))\n- Family health history (certified under a(12))\n\n**Orders:**\n- Medication orders (CPOE)\n- Laboratory orders (CPOE)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders (CPOE)\n- Prescriptions (eRx/EPCS)\n\n**Documents:**\n- Clinical notes and visit summaries\n- Scanned/uploaded documents\n- Faxes (sent and received)\n- Consent forms\n- Patient intake forms\n- Letters and custom forms\n- Education documents\n\n**Financial/Administrative:**\n- Superbill/encounter charges\n- Claims data\n- Fee schedules\n- Eligibility verification records\n- Payment records\n- Account statements\n- Appointment schedules and history\n\n**Quality/Compliance:**\n- Clinical quality measure data (20 CQMs)\n- MIPS/MACRA quality reporting data\n- Audit logs\n- Public health reporting data (immunizations, syndromic surveillance, case reporting)\n\n**Patient-Generated:**\n- Portal-submitted demographics updates\n- Health questionnaire responses\n- Appointment requests\n- Secure messages\n- Post-visit surveys\n- Online payments\n\n---\n\n## 8. Pricing\n\n- **Starting Price:** $275/month per NPI (National Provider Identifier), with some sources listing $299/month\n- **Implementation Fee:** Approximately $2,500 one-time for small practices (1-5 doctors)\n- **Contract:** No lock-in period; month-to-month invoicing available\n- **Pricing Model:** Subscription-based; varies by organization size, features needed, and customization level\n- **Positioning:** Marketed as an affordable option compared to competitors\n\n---\n\n## 9. Customer Feedback and Reviews\n\n### Third-Party Rating\n- **Software Finder:** 4.6/5 stars (19 reviews, 95% positive)\n- **Capterra/GetApp:** Generally positive (exact star rating could not be extracted due to access restrictions, but search snippets indicate positive reception)\n\n### Strengths (Consistently Cited)\n1. **Customer Support** -- This is the most universally praised aspect. Users describe response times of ~2 hours, ability to reach someone by phone immediately, professional and courteous staff\n2. **Ease of Use** -- User-friendly, intuitive interface; tabs easy to find and navigate\n3. **Customization** -- Highly customizable templates for specific practice and specialty needs\n4. **Implementation** -- Quick setup (faster than industry standard of ~3 months); thorough pre-transition analysis; excellent on-site go-live support\n5. **No Contract** -- Month-to-month invoicing appreciated\n6. **Built-in PACS** -- Valued by practices needing imaging\n7. **Cloud Accessibility** -- Access from anywhere on any device\n\n### Weaknesses (Cited in Reviews)\n1. **Patient Portal** -- Described as difficult for patients to understand/use\n2. **Lab Ordering** -- Reported as too complicated\n3. **Calendar Sync** -- Users report inability to sync calendar\n4. **UI Quirk** -- Hovering near side tabs accidentally opens them\n5. **Lag** -- Occasional lag between Nexus and billing/scheduling; occasional lag with e-prescribing\n6. **Comparison** -- Some users note it does not match up to larger competitors like Athena Health or Simple Practice\n7. **Learning Curve** -- Some reports of a steep learning curve for new users\n\n### Notable Testimonials from Vendor Website\n- **Dr. Mathew Sipple** (Psychiatry, Bay City Psychiatry): \"Extremely satisfied with the work of Nexus Support\"\n- **Don Causey** (VP/COO, Costrini Sleep Services, Savannah, GA): \"Initial integration between these two systems was almost flawless\"\n- **Dr. Edward Rhomberg** (Orthopedics, Western Arkansas): \"Customer support by the Nexus team is second to none\"\n- **Dr. Brian K. Nadolne** (FAAFP): Ideal for small practices (1-3 physicians); easy meaningful use attestation\n- **Joan Troy** (Practice Administrator, North Atlanta Surgical Associates): Excellent on-site go-live support\n- **George McNeill** (IT Manager, Fortanasce & Associates Neurology, Arcadia, CA): Built-in PACS, data migration assistance, continuous improvement based on suggestions\n- **Dr. Georgiy Brusovanik** (Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery): \"Nexus Clinical has tied it all together for my practice\"\n\n---\n\n## 10. The company has approximately 10-19 employees across North America and Asia. Leadership includes CEO Pooja Tripathi (25+ years experience), VP of IT Kiran Agate (who led EHR design and development), a billing consultant (Jennifer Somers, dual AAPC-certified CPC/CPPM), and a director of customer acquisition (Sunil Gupta). The company's mission is delivering affordable Healthcare IT solutions to ambulatory medical practices.\n\nNexus Clinical makes one product — Nexus EHR — a cloud-based, unified platform combining EHR, practice management, medical billing, patient portal, telehealth, and eFax. The product targets small to midsize ambulatory practices (solo practitioners up to 50+ physician organizations) across 25+ specialties. Pricing starts around $275-299/month per NPI with no long-term contracts. The company competes against much larger players (athenahealth, eClinicalWorks, NextGen) primarily on price, customer service responsiveness, and willingness to customize. Third-party reviews consistently praise the customer support (response times ~2 hours, phone access) — this appears to be their primary competitive differentiator. The exact customer count is not publicly disclosed; the low number of third-party reviews (~19 on Software Finder) suggests a small install base.\n\n## Product: Nexus EHR\n\nCHPL ID: 11130 (version 7.3, certified 2022-12-27, 34 criteria)\n\n### What It Is\n\nNexus EHR is a comprehensive, cloud-based ambulatory EHR platform. It is a single product — the certified module *is* the whole product. There are no separate product lines or distinct platform editions. The product includes clinical EHR, practice management, medical billing, patient portal, telehealth, and eFax as integrated modules within one system. It is accessible via any web browser or tablet.\n\nThe certification is broad: clinical criteria (a)(1)-(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14) cover CPOE for medications/labs/imaging, drug interaction checks, demographics, family health history, and implantable devices. Care coordination criteria (b)(1)-(b)(3) cover transitions of care, clinical information reconciliation, and e-prescribing. Patient engagement (e)(1) and (e)(3) cover the patient portal and patient health information capture. Public health criteria (f)(1), (f)(2), (f)(5) cover immunization registry reporting, syndromic surveillance, and electronic case reporting. FHIR APIs are certified under (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10). CQM criteria (c)(1)-(c)(3) support 20 clinical quality measures.\n\nIncluded third-party components: MedlinePlus Connect (patient education) and NewCropRx (pharmacy integration/e-prescribing). Optional add-ons: EMR Direct (interoperability engine) and DynaMed Plus by EBSCO Host (clinical decision support).\n\n### Users & Market\n\n**Target users:** Physicians, nurses, lab managers, pharmacists, clinical staff, billing staff, front desk staff, practice managers, and patients (via portal). The product is ambulatory-focused — no hospital/inpatient capabilities were described.\n\n**Practice sizes:** Solo practitioners up to organizations of 50+ physicians.\n\n**Specialties supported (from vendor materials):** Cardiology, Family Medicine, General Surgery, Neurology, Neurosurgery, Orthopedics, Podiatry, Psychiatry, Pain Management, Behavioral Health, Dermatology, Pediatrics, Urgent Care, Pulmonary, Oncology, Primary Care, and more. Testimonials also reference Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery and Sleep Medicine practices.\n\n**Notable deployments (from testimonials):**\n- Bay City Psychiatry (Dr. Mathew Sipple) — Psychiatry\n- Costrini Sleep Services, Savannah, GA (Don Causey, VP/COO) — Sleep Medicine\n- Dr. Edward Rhomberg, Western Arkansas — Orthopedics\n- North Atlanta Surgical Associates (Joan Troy, Practice Administrator) — Surgery\n- Fortanasce & Associates Neurology, Arcadia, CA (George McNeill, IT Manager) — Neurology\n- Dr. Georgiy Brusovanik — Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery\n\n**Market position:** Very small vendor. No customer count disclosed. Competes on price, service quality, and customization rather than market share or brand recognition.\n\n### Modules & Functionality\n\nThe vendor website describes six core modules. The company has approximately 10-19 employees across North America and Asia. Leadership includes CEO Pooja Tripathi (25+ years experience), VP of IT Kiran Agate (who led EHR design and development), a billing consultant (Jennifer Somers, dual AAPC-certified CPC/CPPM), and a director of customer acquisition (Sunil Gupta). The company's mission is delivering affordable Healthcare IT solutions to ambulatory medical practices.\n\nNexus Clinical makes one product — Nexus EHR — a cloud-based, unified platform combining EHR, practice management, medical billing, patient portal, telehealth, and eFax. The product targets small to midsize ambulatory practices (solo practitioners up to 50+ physician organizations) across 25+ specialties. Pricing starts around $275-299/month per NPI with no long-term contracts. The company competes against much larger players (athenahealth, eClinicalWorks, NextGen) primarily on price, customer service responsiveness, and willingness to customize. Third-party reviews consistently praise the customer support (response times ~2 hours, phone access) — this appears to be their primary competitive differentiator. The exact customer count is not publicly disclosed; the low number of third-party reviews (~19 on Software Finder) suggests a small install base.\n\n## Product: Nexus EHR\n\nCHPL ID: 11130 (version 7.3, certified 2022-12-27, 34 criteria)\n\n### What It Is\n\nNexus EHR is a comprehensive, cloud-based ambulatory EHR platform. It is a single product — the certified module *is* the whole product. There are no separate product lines or distinct platform editions. The product includes clinical EHR, practice management, medical billing, patient portal, telehealth, and eFax as integrated modules within one system. It is accessible via any web browser or tablet.\n\nThe certification is broad: clinical criteria (a)(1)-(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14) cover CPOE for medications/labs/imaging, drug interaction checks, demographics, family health history, and implantable devices. Care coordination criteria (b)(1)-(b)(3) cover transitions of care, clinical information reconciliation, and e-prescribing. Patient engagement (e)(1) and (e)(3) cover the patient portal and patient health information capture. Public health criteria (f)(1), (f)(2), (f)(5) cover immunization registry reporting, syndromic surveillance, and electronic case reporting. FHIR APIs are certified under (g)(7), (g)(9), (g)(10). CQM criteria (c)(1)-(c)(3) support 20 clinical quality measures.\n\nIncluded third-party components: MedlinePlus Connect (patient education) and NewCropRx (pharmacy integration/e-prescribing). Optional add-ons: EMR Direct (interoperability engine) and DynaMed Plus by EBSCO Host (clinical decision support).\n\n### Users & Market\n\n**Target users:** Physicians, nurses, lab managers, pharmacists, clinical staff, billing staff, front desk staff, practice managers, and patients (via portal). The product is ambulatory-focused — no hospital/inpatient capabilities were described.\n\n**Practice sizes:** Solo practitioners up to organizations of 50+ physicians.\n\n**Specialties supported (from vendor materials):** Cardiology, Family Medicine, General Surgery, Neurology, Neurosurgery, Orthopedics, Podiatry, Psychiatry, Pain Management, Behavioral Health, Dermatology, Pediatrics, Urgent Care, Pulmonary, Oncology, Primary Care, and more. Testimonials also reference Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery and Sleep Medicine practices.\n\n**Notable deployments (from testimonials):**\n- Bay City Psychiatry (Dr. Mathew Sipple) — Psychiatry\n- Costrini Sleep Services, Savannah, GA (Don Causey, VP/COO) — Sleep Medicine\n- Dr. Edward Rhomberg, Western Arkansas — Orthopedics\n- North Atlanta Surgical Associates (Joan Troy, Practice Administrator) — Surgery\n- Fortanasce & Associates Neurology, Arcadia, CA (George McNeill, IT Manager) — Neurology\n- Dr. Georgiy Brusovanik — Minimally Invasive Spine Surgery\n\n**Market position:** Very small vendor. No customer count disclosed. Competes on price, service quality, and customization rather than market share or brand recognition.\n\n### Modules & Functionality\n\nThe vendor website describes six core modules. Information comes primarily from dedicated pages at nexusclinical.com/features/, nexusclinical.com/nexus-ehr/, and individual module pages.\n\n**1. Electronic Health Records (Core Clinical)**\nSource: nexusclinical.com/nexus-ehr/\n- Single-screen visit documentation — no window switching needed\n- Multi-modal input: preconfigured templates, free text, built-in voice-to-text, Dragon compatibility, transcription services\n- Support for up to 10 simultaneous open patient records without losing unsaved work\n- Rapid charting using conventional formats (superbill, medical history, review of systems)\n- Customizable workflows per visit type\n- Real-time MIPS/MACRA quality measure alerts before chart sign-off\n- Integrated DICOM/PACS for medical imaging\n- eLab and diagnostics center interfaces\n- Plug-and-play camera device compatibility\n- Direct messaging for secure clinical communication\n- Custom specialty templates (25+ specialties, though the specialty pages themselves are thin — mostly restate general features and invite demos)\n\n**2. Practice Management**\nSource: nexusclinical.com/practice-management/\n- Scheduling with configurable appointment types\n- Automated appointment reminders (email/text)\n- Patient recalls and follow-up scheduling\n- Task management\n- Real-time insurance eligibility checks and payment collection at check-in\n- Digital patient intake (contact info, medical history, allergies, visit reasons, consent)\n- Document scanning, importing, and organizing with custom classification\n- Inventory management — track in-house medications and supplies; prescribe and bill directly from inventory\n- Integrated eFax for sending and importing documents\n- Operations dashboard\n\n**3. Medical Billing**\nSource: nexusclinical.com/medical-billing/\n- Integrated with EHR clinical documentation (seamless charge capture)\n- Configurable superbill, fee schedules, and claim workflows\n- Real-time eligibility verification\n- Claims submission and management\n- Revenue cycle management\n- Error minimization in billing processes\n- Timely filing adherence to prevent claim denials\n- Coordinated workflow between front desk, clinicians, and billing staff\n- The billing consultant on the leadership team (Jennifer Somers, dual AAPC-certified) suggests billing is a core competency\n\n**4. Patient Engagement / Patient Portal**\nSource: nexusclinical.com/patient-engagement/\n- 24/7 patient access to health information\n- Review visit summaries and medical documentation\n- View and download medical records\n- View account statements\n- Update demographics, medical history, and allergies\n- Complete intake forms and custom health questionnaires\n- Request appointments online\n- Join telemedicine/eVisit sessions\n- Pay balances through secure online payments (Stripe, Clover integration)\n- Receive automated appointment reminders\n- Complete post-visit experience surveys\n- Secure messaging with providers\n- Online patient registration for new patients\n- Practice-controlled screening of patient-submitted data before acceptance\n- Note: Third-party reviews cite the patient portal as difficult for patients to understand/use\n\n**5. Telehealth**\nSource: nexusclinical.com/telehealth/\n- Powered by Google Meet\n- Cross-platform: iOS, Android, desktop\n- Patients join via link in text or email\n- Providers can chart simultaneously during video visit\n- Integrated with scheduling workflow\n- Automated email/text reminders before visits\n- HIPAA-compliant\n\n**6. E-Prescribing**\nVia NewCrop integration:\n- Electronic prescribing\n- EPCS (Electronic Prescribing for Controlled Substances) with MFA\n- PDMP (Prescription Drug Monitoring Program) integration\n- SureScripts drug history\n- Formulary checking\n- Drug-drug and drug-allergy interaction checks\n\n**Interoperability:**\nSource: nexusclinical.com blog on interoperability, mandatory disclosures\n- FHIR APIs (certified g(10))\n- HL7 integration with third-party practice management systems\n- C-CDA document exchange for transitions of care\n- Custom APIs offered for real-time data exchange\n- Public health reporting: immunization registries, syndromic surveillance, electronic case reporting\n- Payment processor integration (Stripe, Clover)\n\n### Data & Content\n\nBased on vendor feature descriptions, certified criteria, and user reviews, Nexus EHR stores the following categories of data:\n\n**Patient demographics:** Name, DOB, sex, race, ethnicity, preferred language, address, phone, email, insurance information (source: a(5) certification, patient intake features)\n\n**Clinical records:**\n- Chief complaint and history of present illness\n- Medical history (past medical, surgical, family, social) — family health history certified under (a)(12)\n- Review of systems\n- Physical examination findings\n- Assessment and plan / clinical notes\n- Problem lists\n- Medication lists (active and historical)\n- Allergy lists\n- Immunization records\n- Vital signs\n- Lab results (via eLab interfaces)\n- Diagnostic imaging (DICOM/PACS integrated — source: nexusclinical.com/nexus-ehr/)\n- Implantable device list (certified under (a)(14))\n\n**Orders:**\n- Medication orders (CPOE certified under (a)(1))\n- Laboratory orders (CPOE certified under (a)(2))\n- Diagnostic imaging orders (CPOE certified under (a)(3))\n- Prescriptions including controlled substances (eRx/EPCS via NewCrop)\n\n**Documents:**\n- Clinical notes and visit summaries\n- Scanned/uploaded documents (source: practice management page)\n- eFax sent and received (source: practice management page)\n- Consent forms\n- Patient intake forms\n- Letters and custom form templates with auto-fill\n- Patient education materials (via MedlinePlus Connect)\n\n**Financial/Administrative:**\n- Superbill/encounter charges\n- Claims data\n- Fee schedules\n- Insurance eligibility verification records\n- Payment records\n- Account statements\n- Appointment schedules and history\n- Inventory records (in-house medications and supplies)\n\n**Quality and Compliance:**\n- Clinical quality measure data (20 CQMs)\n- MIPS/MACRA quality reporting data\n- Audit logs (certified under (d)(2)-(d)(3))\n- Public health reporting data (immunizations, syndromic surveillance, case reports)\n\n**Patient-Generated Data:**\n- Portal-submitted demographics updates\n- Health questionnaire responses\n- Appointment requests\n- Secure messages between patients and providers\n- They make one product — **Nexus EHR** — a cloud-based, all-in-one ambulatory platform targeting small to midsize practices (solo to 50+ physicians) across 25+ specialties.\n\n**Key findings:**\n- **Single unified product** — EHR, practice management, billing, patient portal, telehealth, and eFax are all integrated modules, not separate products.
